全国卷范文

地点类范文——餐馆

Everyone has his or her own favorite restaurant. If you ask me what my favorite restaurant is, my

answer would be, the Smiling Dragon. This essay will illustrate the reasons why I like it so much as

follows.

Firstly, the food is of good quality and well prepared. For example, they prepare only fresh fish, which

they purchase at the fish market in Dalian. Secondly, the waiting staff is always friendly, polite, and helpful.

Additionally, they satisfy any need you may have efficiently. Therefore, I never feel embarrassed to

invite my family, friends, or foreigners to join me there for a meal.

In conclusion, It is just hard to imagine what my life would be like without Smiling Dragon

restaurant.

My father was always a good role model for me as I was growing up. He's hard-working, patient and

人物类范文

85

understanding. He also gets a good sense of humor and seems to get on well with everybody. Hopefully I

have inherited some of these traits.

I admire my father because I think he brought me and my brothers up well; he was quite strict but

always fair, and he has always been someone I can turn to for advice. I think my father sets a good example

by working hard and has a positive outlook on life. I remember that he used to leave for work early and

came home quite late, but he always made time for me and my brothers.

Therefore, I hold great respect for my father, and he teaches me how to behave well in the my life

and helps to shape my positive personality.

活动类范文

Everyone has his or her own favorite festival. Some like Spring Festival, while others fancy

mid-autumn day. If you ask me what my favorite festival is, my answer would be Spring Festival. It is to be

noted that Spring Festival is one of China's most popular Festival. This festival is on the first day of the

Chinese New Year.

There are several reasons why I like the Spring Festival. First of all, family members who have not

seen each other for some time will get together at this time. People will go through a lot of trouble in order

to be with their families at this time. Secondly, it is a very joyous time of the year, with best wishes from all

around. For instance, there are firework displays, dinner parties, and dumplings at midnight. Thus,these are

reasons why I like the Chinese New Year Festival.

物品类范文

Everyone has his or her own favorite gift. Some like clothing, while others fancy toys. If you ask me

what my favorite gift is, my answer would be the Apple laptop that I got from my father. When I first saw

it, I was taken by its stream-lined and trendy design. I love it so much that I always take it with me

wherever I go.

It really makes my life much easier. Firstly I could chat on video with my friends from a long distance.

Additionally, I could send my assignments and questions to my teacher or search on internet by using my

computer so that I could get a quick guide. I also find it much faster to take down class notes by typing than

writing on a notebook. Therefore, I still have to say that it has brought me a lot of convenience in life. I

want to thank my father for all the things he has given me. These all remind me of his love and that I must

work hard to live up to his expectations.

图表型范文——就业类

You are allowed to write a composition on the topic What Jobs Do College Graduates Want to Take? You

should write your composition based on the chart and the outline given below.

1. 描述下列图表所表达的信息；

2. 谈谈你对此现象的看法；

3. 根据此现象提出建议。

As it can be seen from the chart, the most striking contrast is in the occupation of teaching: 45% of girl

students would like to become teachers after graduation whereas 5% of boys want to do this job. This

indicates that teaching is preferred by females as it provides a more regular and purer life. Women's

patience also fits the job well. Additionally, the chart also points out that 15% of boys want to become

lawyers while 10% of girls preferring this profession. Further, What boys like to do most is to become

managers and the second largest group is to be businessmen. Girls also like to pursue these two professions,

with a percentage of 15% and 25% respectively. This indicates that boys consider working as managers,

89

businessmen and lawyers more demanding and stimulating. It is true that more boys want to show their

talents in these fields. One thing worthy of noting is that the number of girls wanting to do business is not

small. This shows that girls also want to do challenging and exciting jobs.

In conclusion , Boys are to some extent different from girls in their choice as is seen from the graph.

Nevertheless, no matter what job you will choose, it will be more challenging. With the economical

development and the social progress, science and technology will become more and more important.

As it can be seen from the chart, on the top of the list is good manager/boss,which accounts for 85%.

This indicates that good manager/ boss is the most important element in increasing company loyalty. This is

likely due to the positive atmosphere and happy experience that a good manager/boss could provide.

Besides, the health care benefits and retirement benefits takes up 86% and 87% respectively. This indicates

that the health care and retirement benefits play an important role in company loyalty. The reason for this is

that without extra worries the staff can concentrate on the work. Further, the chart also points out that

convenient commute and flexible time are also effective in increasing the company loyalty.

90

In conclusion, it is reasonable to assume that employees' loyalty is of great importance to the

sustainable development of a certain company. Hence, a company should not only recruit excellent staffs

but also spare no efforts to find or cultivate excellent managers.

图表型范文——传统与科技

You are allowed to write a composition on the topic the future of ebooks and traditional paper books You

should write your composition based on the chart and the outline given below.

1. 描述下列图表所表达的信息；

2. 谈谈你对此现象的看法；

3. 根据此现象提出建议。

注：单位：百万

As it can be seen from the chart, the number of paper book readers decreased slowly from 10 million

in 2010 to 8 million in 2013. This indicates that less people like paper books. This is likely due to the

widespread availability of electronic devices such as smart phones, tablet PC and Electronic Reader, which

is much easier for electronic books to be applied and popularized among the masses. Additionally, the chart

also points out that the number of e-book readers has exceeded that of paper book readers since 2011.

Further, the number of electronic book readers increased steadily from 8 million in 2010 to 12 million in

2013. This indicates that more and more people would like to choose electronic books . This is because

compared with paper books, electronic books are characterized by convenience in terms of carrying and

91

saving spaces.

In conclusion , it is reasonable to assume that electronic books will totally replace paper books one day.

However, no matter what kind of books will vanish, the habit of reading books should not be eliminated.

and：conj. 而且；和，与；于是，然后；因此

【例句】：When he returned, she and Simon had already gone...

【句意】：他回来时，她和西蒙已经走了。

as well as：adv. 也，又，既…又…；除…之外（也）；此外

【例句】：It is in his best interests as well as yours

【句意】：这符合他的最大利益，同时也符合你的利益。

along with：一起； 向前； 进展； 到某处； 沿着； 顺着； 靠着…边

【例句】：I'll go along with you.

【句意】：我将随同你一起去。

together with：与；和…一同，连同…；连…带…；偕

【例句】：I am sending you the book, together with all the available comments on it.

【句意】：我把这本书连同所有的评论寄给你。

not only......but also：不但…而且

【例句】：In production, we demand not only quantity but also quality.

【句意】：我们的产品不但要求数量多，而且要求质量高。

in addition：adv. 另外；并且；除此之外；况且

【例句】：In addition, the respondent disclosed professional confidences to one of the patients.

【句意】：另外，被告还向其中一位病人透露了行业机密。

similarly：adv. 类似地；相似地

【例句】：A mother recognises the feel of her child's skin when blindfolded. Similarly, she can instantly

identify her baby's cry.

【句意】：即便是蒙上眼睛母亲也能摸出自己孩子的肌肤。同样地，母亲也能立即听出自己孩子的

92

哭声。

as well as：adv. 也，又，既…又…；除…之外（也）；此外

【例句】：She published historical novels, as well as a non-fiction study of women in the British Empire.

【句意】：她出版了许多历史小说和一部有关英帝国女性的纪实性研究著作。

besides：adv. 此外；而且；以及；更

【例句】：The house was too expensive and too big. Besides, I'd grown fond of our little rented house.

【句意】：这座房子太贵太大了。再说，我已经喜欢上我们租的小房子了。

furthermore ：adv. 此外；而且；再者；与此同时

【例句】：Furthermore, they claim that any such interference is completely ineffective.

【句意】：此外，他们还声称任何这样的干预都是完全无效的。

also：adv.也；同样；并且 conj. 另外

例：Also, it shows newly released systems for the tests in order to bring attention to them.

【句意】：并且，它还在测试中展示了最新发布的系统，从而引起人们对这些新系统的关注。

moreover：adv. 而且；此外；同时；再者

【例句】：The young find everything so simple. The young, moreover, see it as their duty to be happy and

do their best to be so.

【句意】：年轻人觉得每件事都那么简单；而且，他们把幸福视为自己的责任，并努力去实现幸福。

too：adv. 太；也；很；非常

【例句】：We did learn to read, and quickly too

【句意】：我们确实学会了阅读，而且学得很快。

even：adv.甚至； 即使； 更加； 恰巧在…时候

【例句】：He kept calling me for years, even after he got married.

【句意】：多年来他一直打电话给我，甚至是在他结婚以后。

except： conj.除非；[口语]要不是；除…以外

【例句】：We work every day except Sunday.

【句意】：我们除星期天外每天都工作。

except for：除…之外；若不是

【例句】：Apart from [ Except for] a few grammatical mistakes, this composition of yours is well written.

【句意】：除了几处语法错误之外，你这篇作文写得不错。

additionally： adv.此外

93

【例句】：You can pay bills over the Internet. Additionally, you can check your balance or order

statements...

【句意】：可以在网上支付账单。此外，还能查对余额或订货结算单。

first/firstly：adv. 首次；最早，最初；宁愿；优先

【例句】：Certain guidelines can be given. First, have a heating engineer check the safety of the system.

【句意】：可以提供一些指导原则：第一，找位供暖工程师检查系统的安全性。

second/secondly：adv.第二；其次；以第二位

【例句】：The soil is depleted first by having crops grown in it and second by natural weathering and

bacterial action.

【句意】：土壤首先是被种植其中的作物消耗掉，其次受自然风化和细菌活动的侵蚀。

third/thirdly：adv.第三

【例句】：First, interest rates may take longer to fall than is hoped. Second, in real terms, lending may fall.

Third, bad loans could wipe out much of any improvement.

【句意】：首先，利率的下降可能没有希望的那样快；第二，实际贷款额可能会减少；第三，坏账

很可能抵消大部分增长。

first of all：首先

【例句】：First of all, answer these questions

【句意】：首先，回答这些问题。

in the first place：首先，从一开始；压根儿；固；当初

【例句】：In the first place you are not old and in the second place you are a very attractive man.

【句意】：首先你不老，其次你是个富有魅力的男人。

first and most important：首先也是最重要的

【例句】：First and most important, finance is the heart of the market economy.

【句意】：首先也是最重要的一点是，金融是市场经济的心脏。

initially：adv. 最初；开始

【例句】：Initially, they were wary of Simon.

【句意】：开始时他们对西蒙小心提防。

to begin with：首先，起初

【例句】：To begin with he was Prince Charming.

94

【句意】：首先，他是一个白马王子。

then：adv. 然后；那么； 那时；话说回来

【例句】：He sounded sincere, but then, he always did.

【句意】：他听起来很真诚，可是话说回来，他向来如此。

next：adv. 接下去；然后；居后地；依次的

【例句】：Next, close your eyes then screw them up tight.

【句意】：接着，合上双眼，然后再用力闭紧。

earlier： adv.早期（地），初期（地）

【例句】：In the earlier rounds, Ferguson and Dickinson were the standouts

【句意】：在最初的几轮中，弗格森和迪金森表现突出。

afterwards： adv. 之后；以后；继；然后，后来地

【例句】：Afterwards he went abroad.

【句意】：之后他到国外去了。

later：adv. 后来；随后；较晚地；以后，过后

【例句】：He resigned ten years later.

【句意】：10 年后他辞了职。

following this/that：然后，在那之后

【例句】：Following that, we present the factors that determine interaction style based on component type.

【句意】：然后，我们将说明基于组件类型确定交互样式的各个因素。

afterwards ：adv. 之后；以后；继；然后，后来地

【例句】：Afterwards he went abroad.

【句意】：之后他到国外去了。

lately：adv.近来，最近；不久以前

【例句】：I haven't been sleeping well just lately.

【句意】：我就是在最近才一直睡不好觉。

recently：adv.近日；新近；最近，近来；不久以前

【例句】：We received a letter from him recently.

【句意】：我们不久以前收到了他的一封信。

nowadays：adv.现在，当今

【例句】：Nowadays most kids prefer watching TV to reading.

95

【句意】：现在大多数孩子都喜欢看电视而不喜欢读书。

meanwhile： adv.同时，其间

【例句】：Brush the aubergines with oil, add salt and pepper, and bake till soft. Meanwhile, heat the

remaining oil in a heavy pan.

【句意】：把油涂在茄子上，加盐和胡椒烤至松软。与此同时，在厚锅中加热剩余的油。

last but not the least： 最后也是最重要的

【例句】：Last but not the least, a forecast about the hospitals' future development of cost-evaluation is

given.

【句意】：并且展望了我国医院成本管理的发展方向，希望为发展和完善医院成本核算系统有所帮

助。

Before：conj.在…之前；在…以前；比…早些；与其…

adv.先前，从前；在前，在前方

【例句】：Do it before you forget.

【句意】：尽早动手，免得忘了。

Since：adv.以后，此后；后来；（距今几年）以前；（从那时候起几年）以前

conj.因为；既然；自从…以来；自从…的时候起

【例句】：He had been worrying ever since the letter arrived.

【句意】：自从接到那封信后她就一直焦虑不安。

As：adv. 同样地，一样地；例如

prep.作为，以…的身份；如同

conj.由于；像，像…一样；同时，当…时；尽管

【例句】：It's not as hard as I thought.

【句意】：这没有我想象的那么困难。

Until： prep.到…为止；在…以前

conj.到…为止，在…以前；直到…才

【例句】：Until now I have always lived alone.

【句意】：直到现在，我一直独自生活。

at the moment： 当时；此刻，现在，目前

【例句】：I remember now. He arrived just at the moment it happened.

【句意】：现在我想起来了，他恰恰在事情发生的时候到了。

96

When：adv.当时；什么时候；（用于时间的表达方式之后）在那时；其时

conj.如果；在…时；既然

【例句】：Call me when you've finished.

【句意】：你完成后就打电话给我。

Whenever：adv. 究竟什么时候；不论何时

conj.每当；无论何时；随时；无论什么时候

【例句】：You can ask for help whenever you need it.

【句意】：你如果需要帮助随时可以提出来。

as soon as：一…就…；一经

【例句】：As soon as we found this out, we closed the ward

【句意】：我们一发现此事就关闭了病房。

just as：正如；正像；犹如

【例句】：Just as I started to think that I was never going to get well, the illness began to recede

【句意】：正当我开始觉得自己永远都好不了了的时候，我的病开始好转了。

since：conj.因为；既然；自从…以来；自从…的时候起

【例句】：Since I gained weight,I can't get into my best suit.

【句意】：由于我长胖了，我最考究的西服穿不上了。

as ：conj.由于；像，像…一样；同时，当…时；尽管

【例句】：As it was late, we came back soon.

【句意】：由于时间很晚了，我们很快就回来。

so ：conj.（表示因果关系）因此；（表示目的）为了；（引出下文）；（认为某事无关紧要，尤用

于反驳他人的指责时）（口语）

【例句】：There's no petrol, so it's very difficult to transport goods.

【句意】：没有汽油，因此很难运输货物。

because(of)：因为，由于；基于

【例句】：It is happening in this fashion because of the obstinacy of one woman.

【句意】：事情会如此发展是因为一个女人的固执己见。

thanks to：幸亏， 多亏， 由于

【例句】：Thanks to that job I became an avid reader.

97

【句意】：多亏了那份工作我才成了一个喜欢阅读的人。

due to：由于；因为；欠下债[账]，应给予；应归于

【例句】：Mistakes due to carelessness may have serious consequences.

【句意】：由粗心大意造成的错误有可能带来严重的后果。

owing to：由于，因为；多亏

【例句】：Owing to staff shortages, there was no restaurant car on the train.

【句意】：由于缺人手，这趟火车上没有餐车。

the reason why：所以然，之所以

【例句】：The reason why so is quite obvious.

【句意】：何以如此，原因显而易见。

as a result of/as a consequence of ：由于…，因为…

【例句】：As a consequence of your laziness and rudeness, I am forced to dismiss you.

【句意】：因为你的懒惰和粗鲁，我被迫解聘你。

as a result：结果， 因此

【例句】：As a result, the bad thing has been turned into a good one.

【句意】：结果坏事变成了好事。

leads to：导致；把…带到；领到；（道路）通向

【例句】：The new system may be more economic but will lead to a decline in programme quality.

【句意】：新系统可能更合算，但是会降低程序质量。

thus：adv.于是，因此；如此，这样，像这样，例如； 到这程度，到这地步，这么；如下

【例句】：Thus the first worker's league came into being.

【句意】：第一个工人联盟就这样产生了。

therefore：adv. 因此；所以；故；乃

【例句】：He was busy, therefore, he couldn't come.

【句意】：他忙得很，所以没有来。

consequently：adv. 所以，因此；因此，因而；终于，这样；合乎逻辑的推论是

【例句】：Consequently angularities are apt to occur both below and above unconformities.

【句意】：因此，交角往往出现在不整合面的上部或下部。

it follows that：由此得出结论…，因而断定…

【例句】：It follows that we overuse these fuels.

98

【句意】：由此可见，我们过度使用了这些燃料。

thereby：adv.由此，从而；在那附近

【例句】：Thereby, search can be limited to specific XML elements or XML attributes in the document

structure.

【句意】：因此，可以将搜索限制到文档结构中的特定 XML 元素或 XML 属性。

eventually：adv.终究；终于，最后；竟；总归

【例句】：Eventually your child will leave home to lead her own life as a fully independent adult.

【句意】：最后，你的孩子会离开家，作为一个完全独立的成年人过她自己的生活。

for：conj.因为，由于

【例句】：He got an award for bravery.

【句意】：他因英勇受奖。

in that case：如果是那样的话

【例句】：In that case, we won't wait any longer.

【句意】：那我们就不再等了。

hence：adv. 因此；由此

【例句】：The trade imbalance is likely to rise again in 1990. Hence a new set of policy actions will be

required soon.

【句意】：贸易失衡可能会在 1990 年再次加剧。因此需要很快采取一系列新的政策行动。

accordingly：adv. 因此，于是；依据；照着，相应地

【例句】：We have a different background, a different history. Accordingly, we have the right to different

futures.

【句意】：我们有不同的背景和不同的历史。因此，我们有权拥有不同的未来。

however： adv. 然而；可是；无论如何；不管到什么程度

【例句】：This was not an easy decision. It is, however, a decision that we feel is dictated by our duty.

【句意】：这很难抉择。不过，我们觉得自己有义务作出决定。

admittedly：adv. 公认地；诚然，无可否认

【例句】：Admittedly, it is rather expensive but you don't need to use much.

【句意】：它的确很贵，但不需要用得很多。

99

on the other hand：另一方面

【例句】：On the other hand, the idea appealed to him

【句意】：从另一方面来说，这个想法又使他很感兴趣。

despite：prep. 不管；虽有；尽管（自己）不愿意；不在乎

【例句】：Despite a thorough investigation, no trace of Dr Southwell has been found.

【句意】：尽管进行了彻底的调查，还是没有发现索思韦尔博士的任何踪迹。

【例句】：In spite of great peril, I have survived

【句意】：虽然处境非常危险，我还是挺过来了。

though：adv. 不过；然而；可是，但是；话虽这样说 conj. 即使；虽然，尽管；纵然

【例句】：Though she gave no sign, I was sure she had seen me.

【句意】：尽管她没有示意，我还是确信她看见了我。

although：conj.虽然；尽管；但是；然而

【例句】：Although the sun was shining it wasn't very warm.

【句意】：尽管太阳高照，却不很暖和。

【例句】：Please excuse me, but there is something I must say.

【句意】：不好意思，但有些话我必须说。

otherwise：adv. 否则；另外；别的方式 conj. 否则，不然

【例句】：My parents lent me the money. Otherwise, I couldn't have afforded the trip.

【句意】：我父母借钱给我了。否则，我可付不起这次旅费。

yet：adv. 还；但是；已经；又，再 conj. 然而，但是

【例句】：I don't eat much, yet I am a size 16.

【句意】：我吃得不多，然而我是个穿 16 号的人。

instead of：（用…）代替…， （是…）而不是…， （用…）而不用…

【例句】：Instead of staying in the here and now, you bring up similar instances from the past.

【句意】：你没有紧扣眼前所发生的事，而是只提出了一些过去的类似事例。

rather than：而不是；宁可…也不愿

【例句】：We should help him rather than he should help us.

【句意】：我们应当帮助他，而不是他帮助我们。

100

whereas：conj. 然而；鉴于；反之

【例句】：It's not that difficult. As a matter of fact, it's quite easy

【句意】：最后，我们欣然接受政府的声明，并感谢其愿同我们合作的意愿和承诺。

第一部分：阅读判断（第 1—10 题，每题 1 分，共 10 分）

下面的短文后列出了 10 个句子，请根据短文的内容对每个句子作出判断，如果该句提供的是正确的

信息，选择 A;如果该句提供的是错误的信息,选择 B;如果该句的信息文中没有提及，选择 C

A Dog's Dilemma

Finding a babysitter while you go out to work is, for example, an inconvenience. For the African wild

dog, one of the continent's most endangered carnivores, it's a matter of life and death. New research shows

that once packs fall below a certain size, there are not enough animals to both hunt food and stay at home

protecting the young.

The African wild dog has declined drastically over the past century. Habitual loss, persecution and

unexplained outbreaks of disease have all been blamed. Only 3,000 to 5,000 animals remain, and the

species is expected to go extinct within decades if the trend continues.

Other large carnivores such as the spotted hyena face similar pressures, yet are not declining. Now

Franck Courchamp of Cambridge University has found a reason.

The dog's weakness lies in its social

organization.

Within each pack of up to 20 adults and pups, only the dominant male and female breed. The

remaining animals help raise the pups, cooperate to hunt prey and defend the kill from other carnivores.

Because pups can't keep up on a hunt, large packs leave an adult behind to protect them from predators,

which include lions and hyenas. But leaving a babysitter also carries cost. A smaller hunting party is less

able to tackle large prey and to defend the kill. There is also one less stomach in which to carry food back

to the den, and one more mouth to feed when they get there.

Courchamp investigated this awkward trade-off by modeling how the costs of a babysitter change

with decreasing pack size. This showed that packs of more than five adults should be able to feed all the

pups and still spare a babysitter. But with smaller packs, either the hunting or the babysitting suffers, or the

animals have to compensate by increasing the number of hunting excursions-which itself carries a cost to

the pack.

Field observations in Zimbabwe supported the model. Packs of five animals or fewer left pups

unguarded more frequently than larger packs did. There was also evidence that when they did leave a

babysitter, they were forced to hunt more often.

107

A pack which drops below a critical size becomes caught in a vicious circle, says Courchamp, who is

now at Paris-Sud University. "Poor reproduction and low survival further reduces pack size, culminating in

failure of the whole pack." And deaths caused by human activity, says Courehamp, may be what reduces

pack numbers to below the sustainable threshold.

Mammal ecologist Chris Carbone at London's Institute of Zoology agrees maintaining the integrity of

wild dog packs will be vital in preserving the species, he says.

第二部分：阅读选择（第 11~15 题，每题 2 分，共 10 分）

阅读下面短文，请从短文后各题所给的 4 个选项(A、B、C、D)中选出 1 个最佳选项。

Sleeping is very ancient. In the electroencephalographic（脑电图仪的）sense we share it with all the

108

primates （ 灵 长 类 动 物 ） and almost all the other mammals and birds: it may extend back as far as the

reptiles（爬行动物）．

There is some evidence that the two types of sleep, dreaming and dreamless, depend on the life-style

of the animal, and that predators（食肉动物）are statistically much more likely to dream than prey, which

are in turn much more likely to experience dreamless sleep. In dream sleep, the animal is powerfully

immobilized （ 使 固 定 不 动 ） and remarkably unresponsive to external stimuli, dreamless sleep is much

shallower, and we have all witnessed cats or dogs cocking their ears to a sound when apparently fast asleep.

The fact that deep dream sleep is rare among prey today seems clearly to be a product of natural selection,

and it makes sense that today, when sleep is highly evolved, the stupid animals are less frequently

immobilized by deep sleep than the smart ones.

Why should they sleep deeply at all? Why should a state of such deep immobilization ever have

evolved? Perhaps one useful hint about the original function of sleep is to be found in the fact that dolphins

and whales and aquatic mammals in general seem to sleep very little. There is, by and large, no place to

hide in the ocean.

Could it be that, rather than increasing an animal's vulnerability, the function of sleep is to decrease it?

Wiles Webb of the University of Florida and Ray Meddis of London University have suggested this to be

the case. It is conceivable that animals who are too stupid to be quiet on their own initiative are, during

periods of high risk, immobilized by the implacable arm of sleep. The point seems particularly clear for the

young of predatory animals. This is an interesting notion and probably at least partly true.

11. The author states that during dream sleep, the animal sleeps（

）

A. shallowly

C. without moving

B. on one side

D. intermittently

12. It can be inferred from the passage that animals that are prey rarely experience dream because they

第三部分：概括段落大意和补全句子（第 16~25 题，每题 1 分，共 10 分）

阅读下面短文，请完成短文后的 2 项测试任务：(1)从第 16~20 题后所给的 6 个选项中为每段选择 1

个正确的小标题 ( 2)从第 21 ~ 25 题后所给的 6 个选项中选择 5 个正确选项 ,

How We Form First Impression

① We all have first impression of someone we just met. But why? Why do we form an

opinion about someone without really knowing anything about him or her - aside perhaps from a

few remarks or readily observable traits.

② The answer is related to how your brain allows you to be aware of the world. Your brain is so

sensitive in picking up facial traits, and even very minor difference in how a person's eyes，ears, nose, or

mouth are placed in relation to each other makes you see him or her as difference. In fact, your brain

continuously processes incoming sensory information-the sights and sounds of your world. These

incoming " signals" are compared against a host of " memories" stored in the brain areas called the cortex

system to determine what these new signals " mean".

③ If you see someone you know and like at school, your brain says "familiar and safe". If you see

someone new, it says, “new-potentially threatening". Then your brain starts to match features of this

stranger with other " known" memories. The height, weight, dress, ethnicity, gestures, and tone of voice are

all matched up. The more unfamiliar the characteristics, the more your brain may say, “This is new.

I

don't like this person. “Or else,"l'm intrigued. You’re your brain may perceive a new face but familiar

clothes, ethnicity, gestures-like your other friends; so your brain says:

"I like this person. " But these

110

preliminary "impressions" can be dead wrong.

④ When we stereotype people, we use a less mature form of thinking( not unlike the immature thinking

of a very young child) that makes simplistic and categorical impressions of others. Rather than learn about

the depth and breadth of people-their history, interest, values, strengths, and true character-we categorize

them as jocks, geeks, or freaks.

⑤However, if we resist initial stereotypical impressions, we have a chance to be aware of what a person

is truly like. If we spend time with a person, hear about his or her life, hopes, dreams, and become aware of

the person's character, we will use a different, more mature style of thinking - and the most complex areas

of our cortex, which allow us to be humane.

17. Paragraph②

18. Paragraph③

19. Paragraph④

B. Illustration of First Impression

C. Ways of Departure from Immature and Simplistic Impressions

D. Comment on First Impression

F. Comparing Incoming Sensory Information against Memories

Why on earth would an innocent person falsely confess to committing a crime? To most people, it just

doesn't seem logical.

But it is logical, say experts, if you understand what can happen in a police

interrogation（审讯）room

Under the right conditions, people's minds are susceptible（易受影响的）to influence, and the pressure

put on suspects during police grillings （ 盘 问 ） is enormous. (

26

)"The pressure is important to

understand, because otherwise it's impossible to understand why someone would say he did something he

confess.”

Developmental psychologist Allison Redlich recently conducted a laboratory study to determine how

likely people are to confess to things they didn't do. (

27

)The researchers then intentionally crashed the

computers and accused the participants of hitting the "alt" key to see if they would sign a statement falsely

taking responsibility. Redlich's findings clearly demonstrate how easy it can be to get people to falsely

confess: 59 percent of the young adults in the experiment immediately confessed.(

) of the 15 to

16-year-olds, 72 percent signed confessions, as did 78 percent of the 12 to 13-year-olds.

29

)

Both Kassin and Redlich note that the entire "interrogation" in their experiments consisted of a simple

accusation-not hours of aggressive questioning-and still, most participants falsely confessed.

Because of the stress of a police interrogation, they conclude, suspects can become convinced that

falsely confessing is the easiest way out of a bad situation. ( 30

第二部分：阅读选择

11【答案】C

【考点】事实细节题

【解析】本题的依据在第二段。“In dream sleep, the animal is powerfully immobilized and remarkably

unresponsive to external stimuli．”在该句中我们可以找到关键词“immobilized”与“unresponsive”，它们

实际上与“without moving”同义。其他三个选项的内容都与原文不符。

12【答案】A

【考点】推理判断题

【解析】文章在第二段中提到，在做梦的睡眠中，动物是一动不动的，对外界刺激毫无反应。这

样的。情况对被捕食的动物很危险，因为它们如果睡得很死，就有可能被食肉动物吃掉。所以我们

可以推断，这些动物睡觉时很少做梦，因为熟睡会被别的动物逮住。

13【答案】D

【考点】事实细节题

【解析】解题依据就在“There is...no place to hide in the ocean“。海洋中没有可以躲藏的地方，如

果海豚和鲸睡得多，便有被捕杀的危险。因此它们睡得很少。

14 【答案】C

【考点】推理判断题

【解析】本文第一段指出睡眠是非常古老的现象，第二段一开始就提出睡眠的种类，第三段则

提出睡眠的原始功能。三段从不同角度讨论睡眠，因而不包含 sleep 这个词的选项 B 和 D 可以排除。

选项 A 虽然有 sleep，但本文占较大篇幅的二、三两段均未涉及爬行动物睡眠形式的历史，因此可以

排除。

15 【答案】A

【考点】推理判断题

【解析】根据整篇文章的大意可以推断出深度睡眠是动物进化的更高阶段。

年人也十分脆弱( vulnerable)。

方法，因而某种程度上说也是一种理性的决定。

【解析】空格处应填入形容词修饰 ideas。associate with 意思为“和……相关”。

47 【答案】original

【考点】形容词

121

【解析】空格处应该填入形容词修饰名词。original 意思为“原创的”。

48 【答案】 possibilities

【考点】名词复数

【解析】possibility 意思为“可能性”。这里应使用名词复数。

49 【答案】skiing

【考点】动名词

【解析】practice doing sth．意思为“练习做某事”。因此填入 skiing。

50 【答案】 negotiators

【考点】名词复数

【解析】空格处应填入名词。因为谓语动词为 use，因此主语应该是复数形式。

参考范文（略）

第一部分：阅读判断（第 1—10 题，每题 1 分，共 10 分）

下面的短文后列出了 10 个句子，请根据短文的内容对每个句子作出判断，如果该句提供的是正

确信息，选择 A；如果该句提供的是错误信息，选择 B；如果该句的信息文没有提及，选择 C。

Why are the biggest winners in the past decade of trade globalization mostly in South and East Asia,

whereas the biggest losers are mostly in the former Soviet bloc（集团）and sub-Saharan Africa? History is a

partial guide: East Asia has a long trading tradition, lately reinvigorated（给以新的活力）by the Chinese

adoption of market economics. The Soviet Union, on the other hand, was sheltered from free-market forces

for more than 70 years. In Africa, some countries are disadvantaged because of inadequate infrastructure(基

础结构）；many countries have little to trade but commodities, the prices of which have fallen in recent

years.

In some regions, certain countries have suffered by adopting misguided policies, often under pressure

from International Monetary Fund. First among these is Russia, which in the early 1990s tried to embrace

capitalism before first building the institutions that make capitalism work, such as an independent bank

system，a system of business law, and an adequate method for collecting taxes. Encouraged by the IMF, the

World Bank and the U.S. Department of the Treasury ， President Boris Yeltsin's regime privatized the

state-owned industrial sector, creating a class of oligarchs（寡头政治执政者），who, knowing how unstable

conditions were at home, sent their money abroad instead of investing it at home.

In contrast, China, the biggest winner from globalization, did not follow the IMF formula. Of the

former. states of the Soviet bloc, only a few, notably Poland and Hungary, managed to grow, which they did

by ignoring IMF advice and adopting expansionary plans, including spending more than they collected in

taxes. Botswana and Uganda are also success stories: despite their disadvantages, their countries achieved

vigorous growth by creating stable civil societies, liberalizing trade and implementing reforms that ran

counter to IMF prescriptions.

第二部分：阅读选择(第 11~15 题，每题 2 分，共 10 分)

阅读下面短文，请从短文后各题所给的 4 个选项（A、B、C、D）中选择一个最佳答案

As a communication skills trainer, I've met a wide range of people. Some people have trouble

associating with others and have difficulty making friends. So I would like to discuss two key points to

making good friends. The first is to show respect and love for your friends through your words. Try to say

things to others that would feel good to you if you heard them. For example, say things like, “Thanks for

the great time" and "It was really nice to get to know you". If you are not good at speaking, then smiling

and using body language to express your sincerity is also an effective way to communicate. Additionally, I

recommend that when you meet someone new, send them an e-mail or letter before the day is over to

express how nice it was to have met them.

The goal of good communication is to reach another person's heart and form a comfortable

relationship. It is, therefore, based on the premise that you get rid of your worries and become a bright and

positive person. When there is static noise over the phone line, you can't hear the other person clearly, right?

In the same way, when your mind is full of worries and you try to communicate with another person, you

124

cannot give or receive messages accurately. For example, let's say you are still tied to a past wound of being

betrayed by a friend and feeling hurt. Even if you meet someone new and he or she tells you, " Let's be

good friends," you may not be able to accept it because you will hear a negative inner voice saying

something like "I might be betrayed again" or "The person might have a hidden agenda". With this worry in

your mind, you will not be able to make friends.

If you can be refreshed and get rid of your worries, you should be able to sense others' thoughts and

feelings without prejudice and build a relationship of trust. If you really can't let go of your painful past

experience, then try to change your perspective on the experience. For example, try to think that it was a

necessary lesson for you to understand others' pain. If you can think of the positive intention behind the

event, then you will be able to view the experience favorably and feel better.

)

A. how to make yourself happy

B. how to forget the past unhappiness and sorrow

C. how to show respect and love for your friends

D. how to communicate with others

12. The effective way to communicate covers. (

)

A. sending an e-mail or letter

B. showing respect and love for friends with word

C. smiling and using body language or gesture

D. all of the above

)

A. Minds full of worries will do great harm to interpersonal relationship.

B. The writer conducted a research on how people communicate with their friends.

C. Body language is as effective as saying things that will make friends feel better.

D. If you distrust others, then it's difficult for you to make new friends.

)

A. it does take time for someone to forget all about the unhappiness

B. the goal of good communication is to touch another person's heart

C. the goal of good communication is to form a comfortable relationship

D. good communication is based on the premise that you get rid of your worries and become a bright

125

and positive person

第三部分：概括段落大意和补全句子(第 16~25 题，每题 1 分，共 10 分)

阅读下面短文，请完成短文后的 2 项测试任务：(1)从第 16~ 20 题后所给的 6 个选项中为每段

选择 1 个正确的小标题；(2)从第 21~25 题后所给的 6 个选项中选择 5 个正确选项，分别完成

每个句子。

Radar

① Children enjoy shouting at a high wall and hearing the sound come back to them. These

sounds are called echoes(回声). Echoes have given us a number of valuable tools.

② Echo sounding devices were early used in making maps of the ocean floor. Sounds or

ultrasonic( 超 声 的 ) sounds make good tools for determining how deep the water is under ships.

Sometimes echoes from ultrasonic distance finding devices were prevented from working by fish

swimming past or by the presence of large objects.

So ultrasonic devices have been replaced by

other tools.

③ Radar is now a familiar tool. Like many others it was an unexpected discovery. It was first

observed by two researchers, who were studying sound communication. They were sending signals

from a station on one side of a river in Washington DC to a vehicle across the river. They discovered

that their signals were stopped by passing ships. They recognized the importance of this discovery at

once.

④All this was of course just a start, from which our present

radar has developed, The word

“radar,” in fact, get its name from the term “radio detection(检测)and ranging”.“Ranging” is the term

for detection of the distance between an object and the radar set. Today, in our scientific age, it would

be difficult to manage without radar.

⑤ One of the functions of radar is as a speed control device on highways. When a person in an

automobile is driving faster than the speed limit, radar will show this clearly and the traffic police can

take measures to stop him.

⑥ A pilot cannot fly a plane by sight alone. Many conditions such as flying at night and

126

landing in dense fog require the pilot to use radar. Human eyes are not very good at determining

speeds of approaching objects, but radar can show the pilot how fast near planes are moving.

17. Paragraph②______

20. Paragraph⑤______

A. Discovery by Chance

B. Echoes

C. Highway Police

D. Early Use of Echo Sounding Devices

E. Present Radar Is Developed and Used

F. Study of Sound

22. Ultrasonic devices were used to _____

23. Scientific age is_____

24. Police use radar on highways to_____

25. Radar helps pilots to_____

A. decide how fast you drive

B. observe water flow

C. detect nearby objects

D. determine the depth of the ocean water

E. in need of radar

F. map the ocean floor

第七部分：短文写作（第 51 题．30 分）

请根据要求撰写一篇 150 词左右的英语短文。

51. Directions: Write a short essay with a clear central idea and effective development. You should base

your writing on the following situation.

Most citizens in Beijing are pleased with the free bus travel provided to seniors over 65 years old. However,

a heated discussion has been triggered when seniors take the bus at rush hour with the young workers,

because it intensifies traffic jams. Some suggest canceling the free ride for seniors at rush hour while others

state that due respect should be given to seniors. Which side would you take and what reasons can you

offer?

130

第一部分：阅读判断

1 【答案】A

【考点】快速检索信息

【解析】这里的关键词是 South and East Asia，定位到文章的第一句话“Why are the biggest winners in

the past decade of trade globalization mostly in South and East Asia,..”，意思是说过去十几年贸易全球化

中东亚和南亚是最大的赢家，与题干的意思是吻合的，因此题干是正确的。

2 【答案】A

【考点】快速检索信息

【解析】这里的关键词是 trading tradition，在原文中找到相关描述“East Asia has a long trading

tradition...虽然这里没有直接提到日本，但是东亚就包括了日本，所以题干说法是正确的。

3 【答案】A

【考点】快速检索信息

【解析】这里的关键词是 Russia，第二段第二句话 First among these is Russia，which...这里的 these

指的是前面一句话 In some regions，certain countries have suffered by adopting misguided policies，often

under pressure from International Monetary Fund.因此说法正确。

4 【答案】B

【考点】快速检索信息

【解析】 关键词是 African countries 和 IMF formula，相关句子在最后一段话：Botswana and Uganda

are also success stories：despite their disadvantages...that ran counter to IMF prescriptions.博茨瓦纳和乌

干达都是非洲国家，可见并非所有非洲国家都遵守了国际货币基金组织的准则。因此所有非洲国家

都遵守了国际货币基金组织准则的说法是错误的。

5 【答案】B

【考点】快速检索信息

【解析】 关键词 Soviet Union，在原文的第一段话中说 The Soviet Union，on the other hand，was

sheltered from free-market forces for more than 70 years.这里的 sheltered from 是“避开、回避”的意思，

free-market 是“自由市场”，我们知道资本主义国家的市场体系是自由市场，所以苏联不是资本主义

国家。

31【答案】I

【考点】形容词

【解析】feel 是一个系动词，可以判断此处应填入一个形容词，通过上下文意思以及后面的介词

about．可以确定为 guilty，短语 feel guilty about sth．意思是“对……感到有愧”。全句的意思为“我们

很爱吃，但是往往在吃完之后又有负罪感”。

32【答案】A

【考点】固定搭配

【解析】be obsessed with 为固定搭配，意思为“被……缠住／迷住心窍”。全句的意思为“我们很执

着于健 康和减肥，但肥胖却又空前地在蔓延”。

33【答案】E

【考点】名词

【解析】本句缺一个名词作主语，并且根据和介词 to 的搭配，可以推断出正确选项为 answer。

34【答案】F

【考点】形容词比较级

【解析】本句根据 more 和 ways 可以判断出需要填入一个形容词构成比较级，根据上下文，表示“旨

在禁止酗酒的禁酒令，却激发了更多新奇的方法来酗酒”，可以确定 F 为正确选项。

35【答案】D

【考点】过去分词

【解析】根据 be 和 by 确定应填入一个过去分词。再看上下文，上文表示“应该吃典型的美国人吃

的食物”，下文通过 but 转折，表示实际上“美国的食物已经被诸如比萨和热狗这样的舶来品所诠释了”，

因此可以确定 D 为正确选项。

36【答案】 H

【考点】名词词义

【解析】可根据 reasons 来推断此处填 result。

37【答案】J

【考点】名词

【解析】由于横线后面没有宾语，可以确定本句所缺的为一名词，作 bring 的宾语。根据下文解释

“美国人对他们所吃的食物的态度是矛盾的”可以推出本句意义为“坚定的观点也不是确定不变的”。

135

因此可以确定 J 为正确选项。

38【答案】 C

【考点】固定短语

【解析】系动词 become 后应填入一个形容词，和后面介词 of 形成短语 be/become suspicious of，

意义是“对……感到怀疑”。

39【答案】 K

【考点】名词作主语

【解析】本句缺一个名词作主语，并且根据和介词 in 的搭配，可以推断出正确选项为 belief，(have)

belief in sth.意思是“相信……”。

40【答案】B

【考点】动词搭配

【解析】本句是一般现在时，缺一个动词，且和 with 搭配，确定选项为 share，share sth. with sb．意

思是“与某人分享某物”。

第六部分：完形补文

41【答案】accurately

【考点】副词

【解析】本空格处缺少副词修饰动词 judge。accurate 为形容词，词尾加后缀--ly 变成副词，即

accurately。

42【答案】 published

【考点】过去分词

【解析】需填入分词修饰名词 study。由于 publish 和 study 两者间是被动关系，因此填入过去分词

published。

43【答案】 nonverbal

【考点】形容词

【解析】空格处应填入形容词修饰名词 cues。nonverbal 意思为“非言语交际的”。

44【答案】detailed

【考点】形容词

【解析】空格处应填入形容词修饰名词短语 personality traits（性质特征）。

45【答案】stability

【考点】名词

136

【解析】空格处放在形容词后，说明应填名词。

46【答案】judgments

【考点】名词

【解析】空格处所在句子缺名词作主格，根据后面谓语 were 可知，该名词用复数。故应填入

judgments。

47【答案】photographed

【考点】被动语态

【解析】本句考查被动语态。被动语态的 be 动词 were 已经给出，因此填入动词的过去分词形式，

即 photographed。

48【答案】typically

【考点】副词

【解析】空格处应填入副词修饰动词 worn。

49【答案】 agreeable

【考点】形容词

【解析】空格处应填入形容词修饰名词 people，又根据后面 while 从句中形容穿短靴的人(ankle boots)

的性格为 aggressive（有进取心的，好斗的），可知所填形容词的意思应该是与其相反的，即 agreeable

（随和的，友好的）。

50【答案】unaware

【考点】动词

【解析】本句意为“研究人员指出，有些人会通过选择靴子的样式来掩饰他们真实的性格特征，不

过研究人员也指出，志愿者们一般都不会意识到自己对鞋的选择会显示性格的深层意义。”

第七部分：短文写作
